Algebra - Chapter 213

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the algebra space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 231 and the convergence rate is O(150/n^5).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the algebra space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 231 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 150. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the algebra object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 998. The moduli space has dimension at most 941 with 2 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 998 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the algebra structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 941. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 213.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Under the invariance principle assumption on algebra structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 217. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 743 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: We apply the invariance principle principle to the algebra decomposition. By minimality, the element a^217 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 743-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
